[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 5.6 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pgcrypto;\r\nCREATE ROLE anon;\r\nCREATE ROLE authenticated;\r\nCREATE ROLE service_role;\r\nCREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS auth;\r\nCREATE TABLE auth.users (id uuid PRIMARY KEY);\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION auth.uid() RETURNS uuid LANGUAGE sql STABLE AS $$ SELECT NULL::uuid $$;\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.has_role(uuid, text) RETURNS boolean LANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS $$ SELECT true $$;\r\n\r\nCREATE TABLE public.booking_passes (\r\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(), token_hash text NOT NULL UNIQUE,\r\n  student_id uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id), student_email text, student_name text,\r\n  booking_slug text, total_credits int NOT NULL CHECK (total_credits > 0),\r\n  used_credits int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (used_credits >= 0 AND used_credits <= total_credits),\r\n  session_length_minutes int NOT NULL DEFAULT 70, status text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'active\',\r\n  expires_at timestamptz, notes text, created_by uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id),\r\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\r\n);\r\n\r\nCREATE TABLE public.schedule_events (\r\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(), google_event_id text UNIQUE,\r\n  calendar_id text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'primary\', title text, description text,\r\n  location text, start_at timestamptz NOT NULL, end_at timestamptz NOT NULL,\r\n  all_day boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false, status text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'confirmed\',\r\n  html_link text, meet_url text, source text, etag text, last_synced_at timestamptz,\r\n  deleted_at timestamptz, updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\r\n);\r\n\r\nCREATE TABLE public.booking_pass_redemptions (\r\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\r\n  booking_pass_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.booking_passes(id),\r\n  schedule_event_id uuid REFERENCES public.schedule_events(id), google_event_id text,\r\n  start_at timestamptz NOT NULL, end_at timestamptz NOT NULL,\r\n  status text NOT NULL, idempotency_key text NOT NULL,\r\n  student_name_snapshot text, student_email_snapshot text, meet_url text,\r\n  needs_gcal_sync boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false, gcal_state text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'synced\',\r\n  gcal_attempts int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, gcal_error text, gcal_lease_until timestamptz,\r\n  reschedule_count int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, student_note text,\r\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  UNIQUE(booking_pass_id, idempotency_key)\r\n);\r\n\r\nCREATE TABLE public.gcal_sync_state (\r\n  id int PRIMARY KEY, calendar_id text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'primary\', sync_token text,\r\n  last_synced_at timestamptz, last_full_sync_at timestamptz\r\n);\r\nINSERT INTO public.gcal_sync_state(id) VALUES (1);\r\n\r\nCREATE TABLE public.booking_availability_config (\r\n  id int PRIMARY KEY, timezone text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'America/Toronto\', updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\r\n);\r\nINSERT INTO public.booking_availability_config(id) VALUES (1);\r\nCREATE TABLE public.booking_availability (\r\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(), day_of_week int NOT NULL,\r\n  start_time time NOT NULL, end_time time NOT NULL, created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\r\n);\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_booking_availability() RETURNS jsonb LANGUAGE sql AS $$\r\n  SELECT jsonb_build_object(\'timezone\', c.timezone, \'ranges\', COALESCE((\r\n    SELECT jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(\'day_of_week\', a.day_of_week, \'start_time\', a.start_time, \'end_time\', a.end_time))\r\n    FROM public.booking_availability a\r\n  ), \'[]\'::jsonb)) FROM public.booking_availability_config c WHERE id = 1\r\n$$;\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_slot_bookable(timestamptz, int)\r\nRETURNS boolean LANGUAGE sql STABLE AS $$ SELECT true $$;\r\n\r\nCREATE TABLE public.external_work_uploads (\r\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(), student_id uuid, uploaded_by uuid,\r\n  file_name text NOT NULL, file_type text, storage_path text NOT NULL,\r\n  source_platform text, upload_type text, processing_status text,\r\n  booking_pass_id uuid REFERENCES public.booking_passes(id),\r\n  redemption_id uuid REFERENCES public.booking_pass_redemptions(id),\r\n  booking_idempotency_key text, created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\r\n);\r\n-- Observable SQL contract for the native Single Session booking module.\r\n\r\nDO $$\r\nBEGIN\r\n  IF NOT public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(\r\n    \'2026-08-18 13:00 America/Toronto\'::timestamptz,\r\n    \'2026-08-16 10:00 America/Toronto\'::timestamptz\r\n  ) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'anchored Tuesday slot should be bookable\'; END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(\r\n    \'2026-08-18 13:30 America/Toronto\'::timestamptz,\r\n    \'2026-08-16 10:00 America/Toronto\'::timestamptz\r\n  ) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'off-pitch slot should not be bookable\'; END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(\r\n    \'2026-08-18 13:00 America/Toronto\'::timestamptz,\r\n    \'2026-08-17 14:00 America/Toronto\'::timestamptz\r\n  ) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'slot inside 24-hour notice should not be bookable\'; END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(\r\n    \'2026-08-25 13:00 America/Toronto\'::timestamptz,\r\n    \'2026-08-16 10:00 America/Toronto\'::timestamptz\r\n  ) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'slot beyond six-business-day boundary should not be bookable\'; END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF NOT public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(\r\n    \'2026-11-02 14:00 America/Toronto\'::timestamptz,\r\n    \'2026-10-30 10:00 America/Toronto\'::timestamptz\r\n  ) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'DST boundary changed a valid host-local anchor\'; END IF;\r\nEND;\r\n$$;\r\n\r\nCREATE TABLE public.single_session_test_runtime AS\r\nWITH config AS (\r\n  SELECT timezone, (now() AT TIME ZONE timezone)::date AS local_today\r\n  FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1\r\n), candidates AS (\r\n  SELECT ((d.day::date + a.start_time) AT TIME ZONE c.timezone) AS start_at,\r\n    row_number() OVER (ORDER BY d.day, a.start_time) AS n\r\n  FROM config c\r\n  CROSS JOIN LATERAL generate_series(\r\n    c.local_today::timestamp,\r\n    public.single_session_booking_window_end(now())::timestamp,\r\n    interval \'1 day\'\r\n  ) d(day)\r\n  JOIN public.booking_availability a\r\n    ON a.day_of_week = extract(dow FROM d.day)::integer\r\n  WHERE a.end_time - a.start_time >= interval \'5 hours 50 minutes\'\r\n    AND ((d.day::date + a.start_time) AT TIME ZONE c.timezone) >= now() + interval \'24 hours\'\r\n)\r\nSELECT start_at AS base_start,\r\n  (SELECT start_at FROM candidates WHERE n = 2) AS race_start,\r\n  (SELECT start_at FROM candidates WHERE n = 3) AS cross_race_start,\r\n  NULL::uuid AS cross_race_pass_id\r\nFROM candidates WHERE n = 1;\r\n\r\nDO $$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _base timestamptz := (SELECT base_start FROM public.single_session_test_runtime);\r\n  _booking public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _same public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _stale_id uuid;\r\n  _replacement public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _package_pass uuid;\r\n  _job public.single_session_outbox%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _sweep public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _result jsonb;\r\n  _caught boolean;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  IF _base IS NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'test fixture could not find a wide availability range\'; END IF;\r\n\r\n  _booking := public.claim_single_session_slot(\r\n    _base, \'Booker One\', \'BOOKER@example.com\', \'Prepare conditional logic\',\r\n    \'["guest@example.com"]\'::jsonb, \'idem-primary-0001\', repeat(\'a\', 32), repeat(\'i\', 32), NULL\r\n  );\r\n  IF _booking.status <> \'draft\'\r\n     OR _booking.draft_expires_at < now() + interval \'4 minutes 50 seconds\'\r\n     OR _booking.booking_token_expires_at < now() + interval \'23 hours 59 minutes\'\r\n     OR _booking.email <> \'booker@example.com\' THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'claim did not atomically create normalized occupying draft\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  _same := public.claim_single_session_slot(\r\n    _base, \'Booker One\', \'booker@example.com\', \'Prepare conditional logic\',\r\n    \'["guest@example.com"]\'::jsonb, \'idem-primary-0001\', repeat(\'a\', 32), repeat(\'i\', 32), NULL\r\n  );\r\n  IF _same.id <> _booking.id THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'double submit was not idempotent\'; END IF;\r\n\r\n  _caught := false;\r\n  BEGIN\r\n    PERFORM public.claim_single_session_slot(\r\n-- What a bookable event IS, as data instead of as constants.\r\n--\r\n-- The single session\'s shape -- 70 minutes, 24 hours\' notice, no buffers, a six\r\n-- business day window, $45 -- is currently written into the edge function AND\r\n-- into three SQL functions that enforce the slot policy inside the booking\r\n-- transaction. That is why there can only ever be one kind of session: a\r\n-- consultation is 30 minutes with 30-minute buffers and a five day window, and\r\n-- there is nowhere to say so.\r\n--\r\n-- This migration only creates the vocabulary and seeds it with the values that\r\n-- are live today. Nothing reads it yet, so this changes no behavior. The SQL\r\n-- policy functions are switched over to it next, one at a time.\r\n--\r\n-- Every number here was read from Joshua\'s Cal.com account on 2026-08-30, which\r\n-- stays the reference for what these events are supposed to be.\r\n\r\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.booking_event_types (\r\n  slug text PRIMARY KEY,\r\n  label text NOT NULL,\r\n  length_minutes int NOT NULL CHECK (length_minutes > 0),\r\n  -- Null means "start a slot every length_minutes", which is what Cal.com does\r\n  -- when no explicit interval is set. The single session\'s back-to-back 70\r\n  -- minute chain (1:00, 2:10, 3:20) is this rule, not a quirk.\r\n  slot_interval_minutes int CHECK (slot_interval_minutes IS NULL OR slot_interval_minutes > 0),\r\n  minimum_notice_minutes int NOT NULL DEFAULT 1440 CHECK (minimum_notice_minutes >= 0),\r\n  buffer_before_minutes int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (buffer_before_minutes >= 0),\r\n  buffer_after_minutes int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (buffer_after_minutes >= 0),\r\n  -- Business days size the window; days inside it are offered whenever the\r\n  -- weekly hours cover them, Saturday included.\r\n  window_business_days int NOT NULL DEFAULT 6 CHECK (window_business_days > 0),\r\n  price_cents int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (price_cents >= 0),\r\n  currency text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'usd\',\r\n  requires_payment boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,\r\n  active boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,\r\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\r\n);\r\n\r\nINSERT INTO public.booking_event_types (\r\n  slug, label, length_minutes, slot_interval_minutes, minimum_notice_minutes,\r\n  buffer_before_minutes, buffer_after_minutes, window_business_days,\r\n  price_cents, currency, requires_payment\r\n) VALUES\r\n  (\'single-session\', \'LSAT Session\', 70, NULL, 1440, 0,  0,  6, 4500, \'usd\', true),\r\n  (\'consultation\',   \'Consultation\', 30, NULL, 1440, 30, 30, 5,    0, \'usd\', false)\r\nON CONFLICT (slug) DO NOTHING;\r\n\r\n-- One student, one price. Joshua already runs this by hand on Cal.com -- there\r\n-- are twelve event types on that account and most of them exist only because a\r\n-- particular person pays a particular rate. Keyed by email because the booker\r\n-- is not necessarily signed in when they book.\r\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.booking_price_overrides (\r\n  email text NOT NULL,\r\n  event_slug text NOT NULL REFERENCES public.booking_event_types(slug) ON DELETE CASCADE,\r\n  price_cents int NOT NULL CHECK (price_cents >= 0),\r\n  note text,\r\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  PRIMARY KEY (email, event_slug)\r\n);\r\n\r\nALTER TABLE public.booking_event_types ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\r\nALTER TABLE public.booking_price_overrides ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\r\n\r\n-- Students never read these directly: the booking page gets everything it needs\r\n-- from the edge function, which runs as service_role.\r\nCREATE POLICY "Admins manage booking event types"\r\n  ON public.booking_event_types FOR ALL TO authenticated\r\n  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'))\r\n  WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\r\n\r\nCREATE POLICY "Admins manage booking price overrides"\r\n  ON public.booking_price_overrides FOR ALL TO authenticated\r\n  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'))\r\n  WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\r\n\r\nGRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.booking_event_types TO authenticated;\r\nGRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.booking_price_overrides TO authenticated;\r\nGRANT ALL ON public.booking_event_types TO service_role;\r\nGRANT ALL ON public.booking_price_overrides TO service_role;\r\n\r\n-- The price a given booker pays for a given event: their override, else the\r\n-- event\'s own price. One definition, so the checkout and the payment\r\n-- verification can never disagree about what was owed.\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.booking_price_cents(_event_slug text, _email text)\r\nRETURNS integer\r\nLANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\r\n  SELECT COALESCE(\r\n    (SELECT o.price_cents FROM public.booking_price_overrides o\r\n      WHERE o.event_slug = _event_slug AND lower(o.email) = lower(COALESCE(_email, \'\'))),\r\n    (SELECT t.price_cents FROM public.booking_event_types t WHERE t.slug = _event_slug)\r\n  );\r\n$$;\r\n\r\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.booking_price_cents(text, text) FROM public, anon, authenticated;\r\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.booking_price_cents(text, text) TO service_role;\r\n-- The slot policy stops being about one 70-minute session.\r\n--\r\n-- These three functions are what actually decide whether a time can be sold --\r\n-- they run inside the booking transaction, which is what keeps two people from\r\n-- taking the same slot. Every number in them was a literal: 70 minutes in three\r\n-- places, six business days, 24 hours\' notice. booking_event_types now holds\r\n-- those numbers, and these read them.\r\n--\r\n-- Behavior for the single session is unchanged by construction: the seeded row\r\n-- carries exactly the values that were hardcoded here (70 / null interval / 1440\r\n-- / 6). The event defaults to \'single-session\' everywhere, so every existing\r\n-- caller keeps working untouched.\r\n--\r\n-- These are DROPped rather than REPLACEd because a new argument with a default\r\n-- would make the old one-argument calls ambiguous rather than overriding them.\r\n\r\n-- Which event a booking is for. Existing rows are all single sessions.\r\nALTER TABLE public.single_session_bookings\r\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS event_slug text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'single-session\'\r\n    REFERENCES public.booking_event_types(slug);\r\n\r\nCOMMENT ON COLUMN public.single_session_bookings.event_slug IS\r\n  \'Which booking_event_types row this booking follows: its length, notice, window and price.\';\r\n\r\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n-- Window: business days size the span. Days inside it are offered whenever the\r\n-- weekly hours cover them, Saturday included.\r\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\nDROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.single_session_booking_window_end(timestamptz);\r\n\r\nCREATE FUNCTION public.single_session_booking_window_end(\r\n  _now timestamptz DEFAULT now(),\r\n  _event_slug text DEFAULT \'single-session\'\r\n) RETURNS date\r\nLANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE SET search_path = public AS $$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _tz text := COALESCE(\r\n    (SELECT timezone FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1),\r\n    \'America/Toronto\'\r\n  );\r\n  _day date := (_now AT TIME ZONE _tz)::date;\r\n  _target integer := COALESCE(\r\n    (SELECT window_business_days FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _event_slug), 6\r\n  );\r\n  _business_days integer := 0;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  WHILE _business_days < _target LOOP\r\n    IF extract(isodow FROM _day) BETWEEN 1 AND 5 THEN\r\n      _business_days := _business_days + 1;\r\n    END IF;\r\n    IF _business_days < _target THEN _day := _day + 1; END IF;\r\n  END LOOP;\r\n  RETURN _day;\r\nEND;\r\n$$;\r\n\r\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n-- Is this start time sellable: far enough out, inside the window, inside the\r\n-- weekly hours, and landing on the event\'s own slot grid.\r\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\nDROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(timestamptz, timestamptz);\r\n\r\nCREATE FUNCTION public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(\r\n  _start_at timestamptz,\r\n  _now timestamptz DEFAULT now(),\r\n  _event_slug text DEFAULT \'single-session\'\r\n) RETURNS boolean\r\nLANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE SET search_path = public AS $$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _tz text := COALESCE(\r\n    (SELECT timezone FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1),\r\n    \'America/Toronto\'\r\n  );\r\n  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _length integer;\r\n  _pitch integer;\r\n  _local_start timestamp;\r\n  _local_date date;\r\n  _local_time time;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _event_slug AND active;\r\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RETURN false; END IF;\r\n\r\n  _length := _event.length_minutes;\r\n  -- No explicit interval means slots start every length_minutes, which is what\r\n  -- Cal.com does and what produces the session\'s 1:00 / 2:10 / 3:20 chain.\r\n  _pitch := COALESCE(_event.slot_interval_minutes, _event.length_minutes);\r\n\r\n  IF _start_at IS NULL\r\n     OR _start_at < _now + make_interval(mins => _event.minimum_notice_minutes) THEN\r\n    RETURN false;\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  _local_start := _start_at AT TIME ZONE _tz;\r\n  _local_date := _local_start::date;\r\n  _local_time := _local_start::time;\r\n\r\n  IF _local_date < (_now AT TIME ZONE _tz)::date\r\n     OR _local_date > public.single_session_booking_window_end(_now, _event_slug) THEN\r\n    RETURN false;\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  RETURN EXISTS (\r\n    SELECT 1\r\n    FROM public.booking_availability a\r\n    WHERE a.day_of_week = extract(dow FROM _local_date)::integer\r\n      AND _local_time >= a.start_time\r\n      AND _local_time + make_interval(mins => _length) <= a.end_time\r\n      AND mod(\r\n        floor(extract(epoch FROM (_local_time - a.start_time)) / 60)::integer,\r\n        _pitch\r\n      ) = 0\r\n  );\r\nEXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\r\n  -- Invalid timezone/configuration is not permission to sell a slot.\r\n  RETURN false;\r\nEND;\r\n$$;\r\n\r\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n-- The claim. End time now comes from the event\'s length instead of a literal\r\n-- 70 minutes, and the booking records which event it is.\r\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\nDROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.claim_single_session_slot(\r\n  timestamptz, text, text, text, jsonb, text, text, text, uuid);\r\n\r\nCREATE FUNCTION public.claim_single_session_slot(\r\n  _start_at timestamptz,\r\n  _name text,\r\n  _email text,\r\n  _notes text,\r\n  _guests jsonb,\r\n  _client_idempotency_key text,\r\n  _booking_token_hash text,\r\n  _ip_hash text,\r\n  _user_id uuid DEFAULT NULL,\r\n  _event_slug text DEFAULT \'single-session\'\r\n) RETURNS public.single_session_bookings\r\nLANGUAGE plpgsql\r\nSECURITY DEFINER\r\nSET search_path = public\r\nAS $$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _booking public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _length integer := COALESCE(\r\n    (SELECT length_minutes FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _event_slug), 70);\r\n  _end_at timestamptz := _start_at + make_interval(mins => _length);\r\n  _email_normalized text := lower(trim(_email));\r\n  _guests_normalized jsonb;\r\n  _expired record;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings\r\n  WHERE client_idempotency_key = _client_idempotency_key;\r\n  IF FOUND THEN\r\n    PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_booking.start_at));\r\n    SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking.id FOR UPDATE;\r\n    RETURN _booking;\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF _start_at IS NULL OR NOT public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(_start_at, now(), _event_slug) THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'slot_unavailable\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0425\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  IF NULLIF(trim(_name), \'\') IS NULL OR length(trim(_name)) > 200 THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'invalid_name\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0400\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  IF _email_normalized !~ \'^[^@[:space:]]+@[^@[:space:]]+[.][^@[:space:]]+$\'\r\n     OR length(_email_normalized) > 320 THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'invalid_email\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0400\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  IF length(COALESCE(_notes, \'\')) > 4000 THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'notes_too_long\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0400\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  IF length(COALESCE(_client_idempotency_key, \'\')) NOT BETWEEN 8 AND 200\r\n     OR length(COALESCE(_booking_token_hash, \'\')) NOT BETWEEN 16 AND 256\r\n     OR length(COALESCE(_ip_hash, \'\')) NOT BETWEEN 16 AND 256 THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'invalid_capability\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0400\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  IF jsonb_typeof(COALESCE(_guests, \'[]\'::jsonb)) <> \'array\'\r\n     OR jsonb_array_length(COALESCE(_guests, \'[]\'::jsonb)) > 10 THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'invalid_guests\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0400\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(g.email) ORDER BY g.email), \'[]\'::jsonb)\r\n  INTO _guests_normalized\r\n  FROM (\r\n    SELECT DISTINCT lower(trim(value #>> \'{}\')) AS email\r\n    FROM jsonb_array_elements(COALESCE(_guests, \'[]\'::jsonb))\r\n    WHERE jsonb_typeof(value) = \'string\'\r\n      AND lower(trim(value #>> \'{}\')) <> _email_normalized\r\n      AND lower(trim(value #>> \'{}\')) ~ \'^[^@[:space:]]+@[^@[:space:]]+[.][^@[:space:]]+$\'\r\n  ) g;\r\n  IF jsonb_array_length(_guests_normalized) <> jsonb_array_length(COALESCE(_guests, \'[]\'::jsonb)) THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'invalid_or_duplicate_guests\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0400\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_start_at));\r\n\r\n  FOR _expired IN\r\n    UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b\r\n      SET status = \'expired\', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\r\n          terminal_reason = \'authoritative_expiry\'\r\n    WHERE b.status IN (\'draft\', \'checkout_open\')\r\n      AND COALESCE(b.stripe_expires_at, b.draft_expires_at) <= now()\r\n      AND tstzrange(b.start_at, b.end_at, \'[)\') && tstzrange(_start_at, _end_at, \'[)\')\r\n    RETURNING b.id, b.lifecycle_version, b.gcal_event_id\r\n  LOOP\r\n    IF _expired.gcal_event_id IS NOT NULL THEN\r\n      PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(\r\n        _expired.id, \'gcal_delete\', \'system\', \'pending\', \'expired\',\r\n        _expired.lifecycle_version, NULL\r\n      );\r\n    END IF;\r\n  END LOOP;\r\n\r\n  IF (SELECT count(*) FROM public.single_session_bookings b\r\n      WHERE b.status IN (\'draft\', \'checkout_open\')\r\n        AND COALESCE(b.stripe_expires_at, b.draft_expires_at) > now()\r\n        AND b.ip_hash = _ip_hash) >= 3 THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'hold_cap_ip\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0429\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  IF (SELECT count(*) FROM public.single_session_bookings b\r\n      WHERE b.status IN (\'draft\', \'checkout_open\')\r\n        AND COALESCE(b.stripe_expires_at, b.draft_expires_at) > now()\r\n        AND lower(b.email) = _email_normalized) >= 3 THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'hold_cap_email\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0429\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  IF (SELECT count(*) FROM public.single_session_bookings b\r\n      WHERE b.status IN (\'draft\', \'checkout_open\')\r\n        AND COALESCE(b.stripe_expires_at, b.draft_expires_at) > now()) >= 250 THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'hold_cap_global\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0429\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF EXISTS (\r\n    SELECT 1 FROM public.single_session_bookings b\r\n    WHERE b.status IN (\'draft\', \'checkout_open\', \'booked\')\r\n      AND tstzrange(b.start_at, b.end_at, \'[)\') && tstzrange(_start_at, _end_at, \'[)\')\r\n  ) OR EXISTS (\r\n    SELECT 1 FROM public.booking_pass_redemptions r\r\n    WHERE r.status = \'booked\'\r\n      AND tstzrange(r.start_at - interval \'15 minutes\', r.end_at + interval \'15 minutes\', \'[)\')\r\n          && tstzrange(_start_at, _end_at, \'[)\')\r\n  ) OR EXISTS (\r\n    SELECT 1 FROM public.schedule_events se\r\n    WHERE se.deleted_at IS NULL AND se.status <> \'cancelled\'\r\n      AND se.start_at < _end_at AND se.end_at > _start_at\r\n  ) THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'slot_conflict\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0409\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  INSERT INTO public.single_session_bookings (\r\n    start_at, end_at, name, email, notes, guests, client_idempotency_key,\r\n    draft_expires_at, user_id, booking_token_hash, booking_token_expires_at, ip_hash,\r\n    event_slug\r\n  ) VALUES (\r\n    _start_at, _end_at, trim(_name), _email_normalized, NULLIF(trim(_notes), \'\'),\r\n    _guests_normalized, _client_idempotency_key, now() + interval \'5 minutes\',\r\n    _user_id, _booking_token_hash, now() + interval \'24 hours\', _ip_hash,\r\n    _event_slug\r\n  ) RETURNING * INTO _booking;\r\n\r\n  RETURN _booking;\r\nEXCEPTION WHEN exclusion_violation OR unique_violation THEN\r\n  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings\r\n  WHERE client_idempotency_key = _client_idempotency_key;\r\n  IF FOUND THEN RETURN _booking; END IF;\r\n  RAISE EXCEPTION \'slot_conflict\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0409\';\r\nEND;\r\n$$;\r\n\r\n-- Same grants the dropped versions carried: service_role only, never the client.\r\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.single_session_booking_window_end(timestamptz, text) FROM public, anon, authenticated;\r\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(timestamptz, timestamptz, text) FROM public, anon, authenticated;\r\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.claim_single_session_slot(timestamptz, text, text, text, jsonb, text, text, text, uuid, text) FROM public, anon, authenticated;\r\n\r\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.single_session_booking_window_end(timestamptz, text) TO service_role;\r\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(timestamptz, timestamptz, text) TO service_role;\r\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.claim_single_session_slot(timestamptz, text, text, text, jsonb, text, text, text, uuid, text) TO service_role;\r\n'}]

Both paths must use the same host-day advisory lock.\ncross_race_start="$("${psql[@]}" -Atc "SELECT cross_race_start FROM public.single_session_test_runtime")"\ncross_race_pass="$("${psql[@]}" -Atc "SELECT cross_race_pass_id FROM public.single_session_test_runtime")"\ncross_native_sql="SELECT id FROM public.claim_single_session_slot(\'$cross_race_start\'::timestamptz,\'Cross Native\',\'cross-native@example.com\',NULL,\'[]\'::jsonb,\'idem-real-cross-native\',repeat(\'5\',32),repeat(\'6\',32),NULL);"\ncross_package_sql="SELECT redemption_id FROM public.redeem_booking_pass_v2(\'$cross_race_pass\'::uuid,\'idem-real-cross-package\',\'$cross_race_start\'::timestamptz,NULL,\'Cross Package\',\'cross-package@example.com\');"\nset +e\n"${psql[@]}" -c "$cross_native_sql" >/dev/null 2>&1 &\ncross_pid_native=$!\n"${psql[@]}" -c "$cross_package_sql" >/dev/null 2>&1 &\ncross_pid_package=$!\nwait "$cross_pid_native"\ncross_status_native=$?\nwait "$cross_pid_package"\ncross_status_package=$?\nset -e\nif { test "$cross_status_native" -eq 0 && test "$cross_status_package" -eq 0; } || \\\n   { test "$cross_status_native" -ne 0 && test "$cross_status_package" -ne 0; }; then\n  echo "cross-engine claim race did not produce exactly one winner" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\ncross_native_count="$("${psql[@]}" -Atc "SELECT count(*) FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE client_idempotency_key=\'idem-real-cross-native\'")"\ncross_package_count="$("${psql[@]}" -Atc "SELECT count(*) FROM public.booking_pass_redemptions WHERE booking_pass_id=\'$cross_race_pass\' AND idempotency_key=\'idem-real-cross-package\'")"\ncross_used="$("${psql[@]}" -Atc "SELECT used_credits FROM public.booking_passes WHERE id=\'$cross_race_pass\'")"\ntest $((cross_native_count + cross_package_count)) -eq 1\ntest "$cross_used" = "$cross_package_count"\n\n"${psql[@]}" -f "$repo_dir/supabase/tests/booking/phase_concurrency.sql" >/dev/null\n\ncancel_sql="SELECT public.cancel_booking_redemption(\'20000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001\', true, NULL);"\n"${psql[@]}" -c "$cancel_sql" >/dev/null &\nfirst_pid=$!\n"${psql[@]}" -c "$cancel_sql" >/dev/null &\nsecond_pid=$!\nwait "$first_pid"\nwait "$second_pid"\n\nresult="$("${psql[@]}" -Atc "SELECT used_credits || \':\' || status FROM public.booking_passes WHERE id=\'10000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001\'")"\nrefund="$("${psql[@]}" -Atc "SELECT credit_refunded::text || \':\' || status || \':\' || calendar_action FROM public.booking_pass_redemptions WHERE id=\'20000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001\'")"\ntest "$result" = "0:active"\ntest "$refund" = "true:cancelled:delete"\n\necho "booking database migrations and double-cancel concurrency: PASS"\n\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 5.5 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "-- Booking remediation Phase 2: atomic cancellation and pass administration.\r\n\r\nALTER TABLE public.booking_pass_redemptions\r\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS calendar_action text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'none',\r\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS calendar_status text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'synced',\r\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS calendar_attempts int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\r\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS calendar_error text,\r\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS calendar_lease_until timestamptz,\r\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS calendar_last_synced_at timestamptz,\r\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS cancelled_at timestamptz,\r\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS cancelled_by uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,\r\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS credit_refunded boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false;\r\n\r\nDO $$\r\nBEGIN\r\n  IF NOT EXISTS (\r\n    SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint\r\n    WHERE conrelid = 'public.booking_pass_redemptions'::regclass\r\n      AND conname = 'booking_redemptions_calendar_action_ck'\r\n  ) THEN\r\n    ALTER TABLE public.booking_pass_redemptions\r\n      ADD CONSTRAINT booking_redemptions_calendar_action_ck\r\n      CHECK (calendar_action IN ('create', 'update', 'delete', 'none'));\r\n  END IF;\r\n  IF NOT EXISTS (\r\n    SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint\r\n    WHERE conrelid = 'public.booking_pass_redemptions'::regclass\r\n      AND conname = 'booking_redemptions_calendar_status_ck'\r\n  ) THEN\r\n    ALTER TABLE public.booking_pass_redemptions\r\n      ADD CONSTRAINT booking_redemptions_calendar_status_ck\r\n      CHECK (calendar_status IN ('pending', 'processing', 'synced', 'failed'));\r\n  END IF;\r\n  IF NOT EXISTS (\r\n    SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint\r\n    WHERE conrelid = 'public.booking_pass_redemptions'::regclass\r\n      AND conname = 'booking_redemptions_calendar_attempts_ck'\r\n  ) THEN\r\n    ALTER TABLE public.booking_pass_redemptions\r\n      ADD CONSTRAINT booking_redemptions_calendar_attempts_ck\r\n      CHECK (calendar_attempts >= 0);\r\n  END IF;\r\nEND $$;\r\n\r\nUPDATE public.booking_pass_redemptions\r\nSET calendar_action = CASE\r\n      WHEN status = 'booked' AND (gcal_state = 'pending' OR needs_gcal_sync) THEN 'create'\r\n      ELSE 'none'\r\n    END,\r\n    calendar_status = CASE\r\n      WHEN gcal_state = 'pending' OR needs_gcal_sync THEN 'pending'\r\n      WHEN gcal_state = 'failed' THEN 'failed'\r\n      ELSE 'synced'\r\n    END,\r\n    calendar_error = COALESCE(calendar_error, gcal_error),\r\n    calendar_last_synced_at = CASE WHEN gcal_state = 'synced' THEN updated_at ELSE NULL END;\r\n\r\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS booking_redemptions_calendar_work\r\n  ON public.booking_pass_redemptions (calendar_status, calendar_lease_until, updated_at)\r\n  WHERE calendar_action <> 'none';\r\n\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.cancel_booking_redemption(\r\n  _redemption_id uuid,\r\n  _refund boolean DEFAULT true,\r\n  _actor_id uuid DEFAULT NULL\r\n) RETURNS jsonb\r\nLANGUAGE plpgsql\r\nSECURITY DEFINER\r\nSET search_path = public\r\nAS $$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _redemption public.booking_pass_redemptions%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _pass public.booking_passes%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _did_refund boolean := false;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  SELECT * INTO _redemption\r\n  FROM public.booking_pass_redemptions\r\n  WHERE id = _redemption_id\r\n  FOR UPDATE;\r\n\r\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'redemption_not_found' USING ERRCODE = 'P0404';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  SELECT * INTO _pass\r\n  FROM public.booking_passes\r\n  WHERE id = _redemption.booking_pass_id\r\n  FOR UPDATE;\r\n\r\n  IF _redemption.status = 'cancelled' THEN\r\n    RETURN jsonb_build_object(\r\n      'redemption', to_jsonb(_redemption),\r\n      'pass', to_jsonb(_pass),\r\n      'was_existing', true,\r\n      'refunded', _redemption.credit_refunded\r\n    );\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF _redemption.status <> 'booked' THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'redemption_not_booked:%', _redemption.status USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF COALESCE(_refund, true) AND NOT _redemption.credit_refunded AND _pass.used_credits > 0 THEN\r\n    UPDATE public.booking_passes\r\n    SET used_credits = used_credits - 1,\r\n        status = CASE\r\n          WHEN status = 'exhausted'\r\n            AND (expires_at IS NULL OR expires_at > now()) THEN 'active'\r\n          ELSE status\r\n        END\r\n    WHERE id = _pass.id\r\n    RETURNING * INTO _pass;\r\n    _did_refund := true;\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  UPDATE public.booking_pass_redemptions\r\n  SET status = 'cancelled',\r\n      cancelled_at = COALESCE(cancelled_at, now()),\r\n      cancelled_by = COALESCE(cancelled_by, _actor_id),\r\n      credit_refunded = credit_refunded OR _did_refund,\r\n      -- Even an ID-less row may have a committed Google event whose database\r\n      -- persistence failed. The reconciler searches by redemption stamp before\r\n      -- deciding there is nothing to delete.\r\n      calendar_action = 'delete',\r\n      calendar_status = 'pending',\r\n      calendar_error = NULL,\r\n      calendar_lease_until = NULL,\r\n      needs_gcal_sync = true,\r\n      gcal_state = 'pending',\r\n      gcal_error = NULL\r\n  WHERE id = _redemption.id\r\n  RETURNING * INTO _redemption;\r\n\r\n  UPDATE public.schedule_events\r\n  SET status = 'cancelled', deleted_at = COALESCE(deleted_at, now())\r\n  WHERE id = _redemption.schedule_event_id\r\n     OR (_redemption.google_event_id IS NOT NULL AND google_event_id = _redemption.google_event_id);\r\n\r\n  RETURN jsonb_build_object(\r\n    'redemption', to_jsonb(_redemption),\r\n    'pass', to_jsonb(_pass),\r\n    'was_existing', false,\r\n    'refunded', _did_refund\r\n  );\r\nEND;\r\n$$;\r\n\r\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.cancel_booking_redemption(uuid, boolean, uuid) FROM public, anon, authenticated;\r\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.cancel_booking_redemption(uuid, boolean, uuid) TO service_role;\r\n\r\n"}]

excluded.ranges, active = excluded.active, updated_at = now();\n+    END IF;\n+  ELSE\n+    UPDATE public.booking_passes SET status = _original.before_state->>'status',\n+      expires_at = := CASE split_part(_original.kind, '.', 1)\n+    WHEN 'event' THEN (SELECT to_jsonb(t) FROM public.booking_event_types t WHERE t.slug = _original.target->>'slug')\n+    WHEN 'availability' THEN (SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(a) - 'id' - 'created_at' ORDER BY a.day_of_week, a.start_time), '[]'::jsonb) FROM public.booking_availability a WHERE a.schedule_id = (_original.target->>'schedule_id')::uuid)\n+    WHEN 'override' THEN (SELECT to_jsonb(o) FROM public.booking_date_overrides o WHERE o.schedule_id = (_original.target->>'schedule_id')::uuid AND public.scheduling_audit_log\n+    (actor_id, interface, device_name, kind, target, before_state, after_state, undo_of)\n+  VALUES (_actor_id, _interface, _device_name, 'undo.' || _original.kind, _original.target,\n+    _original.after_state, COALESCE(_current, 'null'::jsonb), _original.id)\n+  RETURNING id INTO _new_id;\n+  RETURN jsonb_build_object('audit_id', _new_id, 'undo_of', _original.id, 'after', _current);\n+END;\n+$$;\n+\n+-- A date override replaces weekly hours. Conflict checks remain in the claim RPC.\n+CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(\n+  _start_at timestamptz, _now timestamptz DEFAULT now(), _event_slug text DEFAULT 'single-session'\n+) RETURNS boolean LANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE SET search_path = public AS $$\n+DECLARE\n+  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\n+  _tz text;\n+  _length int;\n+  _pitch int;\n+  _local_start timestamp;\n+  _local_date date;\n+  _local_time time;\n+  _override public.booking_date_overrides%ROWTYPE;\n+BEGIN\n+  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _event_slug AND active;\n+  IF NOT FOUND THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n+  SELECT timezone INTO _tz FROM public.booking_schedules WHERE id = _event.schedule_id;\n+  _tz := COALESCE(_tz, 'America/Toronto');\n+  _length := _event.length_minutes;\n+  _pitch := COALESCE(_event.slot_interval_minutes, _length);\n+  IF _start_at IS NULL OR _start_at < _now + make_interval(mins => _event.minimum_notice_minutes) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n+  _local_start := _start_at AT TIME ZONE _tz;\n+  _local_date := _local_start::date;\n+  _local_time := _local_start::time;\n+  IF _local_date < (_now AT TIME ZONE _tz)::date OR _local_date > public.single_session_booking_window_end(_now, _event_slug) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n+  SELECT * INTO _override FROM public.booking_date_overrides\n+  WHERE schedule_id = _event.schedule_id AND local_date = _local_date AND active;\n+  IF FOUND THEN\n+    IF _override.closed THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n+    RETURN EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM jsonb_array_elements(_override.ranges) r\n+      WHERE _local_time >= (r->>'start_time')::time\n+        AND _local_time + make_interval(mins => _length) <= (r->>'end_time')::time\n+        AND mod(floor(extract(epoch FROM (_local_time - (r->>'start_time')::time)) / 60)::int, _pitch) = 0);\n+  END IF;\n+  RETURN EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.booking_availability a\n+    WHERE a.schedule_id = _event.schedule_id AND a.day_of_week = extract(dow FROM _local_date)::int\n+      AND _local_time >= a.start_time AND _local_time + make_interval(mins => _length) <= a.end_time\n+      AND mod(floor(extract(epoch FROM (_local_time - a.start_time)) / 60)::int, _pitch) = 0);\n+EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN RETURN false;\n+END;\n+$$;\n+\n+ALTER TABLE public.booking_schedules ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n+ALTER TABLE public.booking_date_overrides ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n+ALTER TABLE public.scheduling_audit_log ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n+ALTER TABLE public.scheduling_previews ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n+\n+REVOKE ALL ON public.booking_schedules, public.booking_date_overrides,\n+  public.scheduling_audit_log, public.scheduling_previews FROM public, anon, authenticated;\n+GRANT ALL ON public.booking_schedules, public.booking_date_overrides,\n+  public.scheduling_audit_log, public.scheduling_previews TO service_role;\n+REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.scheduling_preview(jsonb),\n+  public.apply_scheduling_action(jsonb, uuid, uuid, text, text),\n+  public.undo_scheduling_audit(uuid, uuid, text, text) FROM public, anon, authenticated;\n+GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.scheduling_preview(jsonb),\n+  public.apply_scheduling_action(jsonb, uuid, uuid, text, text),\n+  public.undo_scheduling_audit(uuid, uuid, text, text) TO service_role;\n*** End Patch";
text(await tools.apply_patch(patch));

const patch = "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu-wt-scheduling\\supabase\\tests\\booking\\single_session_lifecycle.sql\n@@\n -- Observable SQL contract for the native Single Session booking module.\n \n+INSERT INTO public.scheduling_audit_log (\n+  actor_id, interface, device_name, kind, target, before_state, after_state\n+) VALUES (\n+  '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000003', 'cli', 'test', 'test.seed',\n+  '{}'::jsonb, '{}'::jsonb, '{}'::jsonb\n+);\n SELECT public.assert_raises(\n@@\n   'preview_not_found'\n );\n+\n+DO $$\n+DECLARE\n+  _preview jsonb;\n+  _applied jsonb;\n+  _undone jsonb;\n+  _original public.scheduling_audit_log%ROWTYPE;\n+BEGIN\n+  _preview := public.scheduling_preview(\n+    '{\"kind\":\"event.update\",\"slug\":\"single-session\",\"values\":{\"price_cents\":4600}}'::jsonb\n+  );\n+  _applied := public.apply_scheduling_action(\n+    '{\"kind\":\"event.update\",\"slug\":\"single-session\",\"values\":{\"price_cents\":4600}}'::jsonb,\n+    (_preview->>'token')::uuid, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000003', 'cli', 'test'\n+  );\n+  SELECT * INTO _original FROM public.scheduling_audit_log WHERE id = (_applied->>'audit_id')::uuid;\n+  _undone := public.undo_scheduling_audit(\n+    _original.id, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000003', 'cli', 'test'\n+  );\n+  IF (SELECT price_cents FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = 'single-session') <> 4500 THEN\n+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'undo did not restore event state';\n+  END IF;\n+  IF NOT EXISTS (\n+    SELECT 1 FROM public.scheduling_audit_log\n+    WHERE id = (_undone->>'audit_id')::uuid AND undo_of = _original.id\n+  ) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'undo did not append linked audit entry'; END IF;\n+  IF NOT EXISTS (\n+    SELECT 1 FROM public.scheduling_audit_log\n+    WHERE id = _original.id AND undo_of IS NULL AND after_state = _original.after_state\n+  ) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'undo mutated original audit entry'; END IF;\n+END;\n+$$;\n*** Update File: C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu-wt-scheduling\\supabase\\migrations\\20260914000000_scheduling_control_center.sql\n@@\n     WHEN 'override.deactivate' THEN\n       UPDATE public.booking_date_overrides SET active = false, updated_at = now()\n       WHERE schedule_id = (_action->>'schedule_id')::uuid AND local_date = (_action->>'local_date')::date;\n+    WHEN 'pass.create' THEN\n+      INSERT INTO public.booking_passes (\n+        student_id, student_email, student_name, token_hash, booking_slug,\n+        total_credits, session_length_minutes, status, expires_at, notes, created_by\n+      ) VALUES (\n+        (_values->>'student_id')::uuid, _values->>'student_email', _values->>'student_name',\n+        _values->>'token_hash', _values->>'booking_slug', (_values->>'total_credits')::int,\n+        COALESCE((_values->>'session_length_minutes')::int, 70), 'active',\n+        (_values->>'expires_at')::timestamptz, _values->>'notes', _actor_id\n+      );\n     WHEN 'pass.update' THEN\n*** End Patch";
text(await tools.apply_patch(patch));

## assistant

The implementation now has the intended shape: previews bind the exact canonical action and current “before” snapshot; applies consume the token once; undo restores eligible configuration and inserts a separate `undo.*` audit row with `undo_of`, leaving the original row immutable. I also added a lifecycle assertion that proves that linkage and restoration behavior.

## assistant

The review caught three real integrity gaps, so I’m tightening the contract before it becomes an API dependency: previews will be operator-bound and target-serialized, undo will reject stale history, and “undo create” will deactivate or revoke instead of deleting. I’m adding lifecycle tests for each boundary as I change it.

const patch = "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu-wt-scheduling\\supabase\\migrations\\20260914000000_scheduling_control_center.sql\n@@\n CREATE FUNCTION public.scheduling_target_lock_key(_action jsonb)\n-RETURNS bigint LANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS $$\n-  SELECT ('x' || substr(md5((_action - 'values')::text), 1, 16))::bit(64)::bigint;\n+RETURNS bigint LANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE AS $$\n+DECLARE\n+  _identity text;\n+BEGIN\n+  _identity := CASE split_part(_action->>'kind', '.', 1)\n+    WHEN 'event' THEN 'event:' || COALESCE(_action->>'slug', '')\n+    WHEN 'pass' THEN 'pass:' || COALESCE(_action->>'id', '')\n+    WHEN 'booking' THEN 'booking:' || COALESCE(_action->>'id', '')\n+    WHEN 'availability' THEN 'availability:' || COALESCE(_action->>'schedule_id', '')\n+    WHEN 'override' THEN 'override:' || COALESCE(_action->>'schedule_id', '') || ':' || COALESCE(_action->>'local_date', '')\n+    ELSE 'invalid:' || (_action - 'values')::text\n+  END;\n+  RETURN ('x' || substr(md5(_identity), 1, 16))::bit(64)::bigint;\n+END;\n $$;\n@@\n   CASE split_part(_kind, '.', 1)\n@@\n       SELECT to_jsonb(r) INTO _result\n       FROM public.booking_pass_redemptions r WHERE r.id = (_action->>'id')::uuid;\n   END CASE;\n+\n+  IF _kind IN ('event.create', 'pass.create') AND _result IS NOT NULL THEN\n+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'scheduling_target_already_exists';\n+  END IF;\n+  IF _kind IN (\n+    'event.update', 'event.deactivate', 'override.deactivate',\n+    'pass.update', 'pass.revoke', 'booking.cancel', 'booking.reschedule', 'booking.refund'\n+  ) AND _result IS NULL THEN\n+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'scheduling_target_not_found';\n+  END IF;\n+  IF _kind = 'availability.replace' AND NOT EXISTS (\n+    SELECT 1 FROM public.booking_schedules WHERE id = _schedule_id\n+  ) THEN\n+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'scheduling_target_not_found';\n+  END IF;\n   RETURN COALESCE(_result, 'null'::jsonb);\n END;\n $$;\n*** End Patch";

public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(\r\n  _start_at timestamptz,\r\n  _now timestamptz DEFAULT now()\r\n) RETURNS boolean\r\nLANGUAGE plpgsql\r\nSTABLE\r\nSET search_path = public\r\nAS $$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _tz text := COALESCE(\r\n    (SELECT timezone FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1),\r\n    'America/Toronto'\r\n  );\r\n  _local_start timestamp;\r\n  _local_date date;\r\n  _local_time time;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  IF _start_at IS NULL OR _start_at < _now + interval '24 hours' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\r\n\r\n  _local_start := _start_at AT TIME ZONE _tz;\r\n  _local_date := _local_start::date;\r\n  _local_time := _local_start::time;\r\n\r\n  IF _local_date < (_now AT TIME ZONE _tz)::date\r\n     OR _local_date > public.single_session_booking_window_end(_now) THEN\r\n    RETURN false;\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  RETURN EXISTS (\r\n    SELECT 1\r\n    FROM public.booking_availability a\r\n    WHERE a.day_of_week = extract(dow FROM _local_date)::integer\r\n      AND _local_time >= a.start_time\r\n      AND _local_time + interval '70 minutes' <= a.end_time\r\n      AND mod(\r\n        floor(extract(epoch FROM (_local_time - a.start_time)) / 60)::integer,\r\n        70\r\n      ) = 0\r\n  );\r\nEXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\r\n  -- Invalid timezone/configuration is not permission to sell a slot.\r\n  RETURN false;\r\nEND;\r\n$$;\r\n\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(\r\n  _booking_id uuid,\r\n  _action text,\r\n  _recipient text,\r\n  _state text,\r\n  _precondition_status text,\r\n  _precondition_lifecycle_version integer,\r\n  _payload_ciphertext text DEFAULT NULL\r\n) RETURNS public.single_session_outbox\r\nLANGUAGE plpgsql\r\nSECURITY DEFINER\r\nSET search_path = public\r\nAS $$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _job public.single_session_outbox%ROWTYPE;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  INSERT INTO public.single_session_outbox (\r\n    booking_id, action, recipient, state, idempotency_key,\r\n    precondition_status, precondition_lifecycle_version, payload_ciphertext\r\n  ) VALUES (\r\n    _booking_id, _action, COALESCE(NULLIF(_recipient, ''), 'system'), _state,\r\n    _booking_id::text || ':' || _action || ':' || COALESCE(NULLIF(_recipient, ''), 'system'),\r\n    _precondition_status, _precondition_lifecycle_version, _payload_ciphertext\r\n  )\r\n"}]

## assistant

The anchored-slot failure came from the minimal test schema having no weekly availability rows. The production migration seeds those hours, but the harness fixture had omitted them. I restored the exact production weekly schedule in the minimal schema, then fixed a second latent fixture dependency the now-running test exposed. The full database lifecycle now passes.

Database tests now pass.

Root cause: the minimal test schema created `booking_availability` but never seeded the production weekly hours. The schedule-aware slot function therefore correctly found no Tuesday availability. I added the exact production schedule rows.

The next run exposed a latent fixture dependency on two users and the shared test booking pass, so I added those existing fixture identities to the minimal schema as well.
